Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
PDpatientFallingEventObservation(?e) ^
RecognizedEvent(?e) ^
hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?f) ^
FeatureOfInterest(?f) ^
hasPatient(?f, ?p) ^
PD_Patient(?p) ^
NotifyNurse(?n1) ^
AlertingNotification(?n1) ^
NotifyRelatives(?n2) ^
AlertingNotification(?n2) ^
sendNotification(?n1, true) ^
sendNotification(?n2, true) ->
sendNotification(?n1, true), sendNotification(?n2, true)

        <rdfs:label>Daily Dosage Plan</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^
schema:Person(?p) ^
hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^
DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt) ^
swrlb:equal(?dt, "4 hours") ^
hasDosagePlan(?p, ?dp) ^
RecognizedEvent(?re) ^
hasFrequency(?re, ?freq) ^
swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinesiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
swrlb:withinTimeInterval(?re, ?dp) ->
sendNotification(?p, ?doctor) ^
hasNotificationRecipient(?notification, ?doctor)

    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 `PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ hasPatient(?e, ?p) ^ PatientWithParkinsonDisease(?p) -> NotifyDoctor(?n) ^ hasNotificationFor(?n, ?p)`

    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 `PDpatient(?p) ^ hasDiagnosis(?p, ?d) ^ Diagnosis(?d) ^ hasDisease(?d, "Parkinson") ^ DailyDosagePlan(?ddp) ^ hasDailyDosagePlan(?p, ?ddp) ^ hasDosageTime(?ddp, ?dt) ^ DosageTime(?dt) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?mdeo) ^ observedForPatient(?mdeo, ?p) -> NotifyDoctor(?n) ^ notifyForEvent(?n, ?mdeo)`

        <rdfs:label>PD patient recognized event observation</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?o) ^ obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ hasTremorObservation(?o, ?t) ^ swrlb:equal(?t, true) -> Notification(?n) ^ hasRecipient(?n, ?r) ^ Relative(?r) ^ hasRelative(?p, ?r) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ recognizedEvent(?e, ?o)`

        <rdfs:label xml:lang="en">Smart Watch</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^
schema:Person(?p) ^
hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^
DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt) ^
swrlb:equal(?dt, "4 hours") ^
hasDosagePlan(?p, ?dp) ^
RecognizedEvent(?re) ^
hasFrequency(?re, ?freq) ^
swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinesiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
swrlb:withinTimeInterval(?re, ?dp) ->
sendNotification(?p, ?doctor) ^
hasNotificationRecipient(?notification, ?doctor)

        <rdfs:comment>PD patient</rdfs:comment>

    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^
Monitored(?p) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^
hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^
containsDopamine(?mp, true) ->
ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

        <rdfs:label>PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^
Monitored(?p) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^
hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^
containsDopamine(?mp, true) ->
ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 `PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ hasPatient(?e, ?p) ^ PatientWithParkinsonDisease(?p) -> NotifyDoctor(?n) ^ hasNotificationFor(?n, ?p)`

    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?o) ^ obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ hasTremorObservation(?o, ?t) ^ swrlb:equal(?t, true) -> Notification(?n) ^ hasRecipient(?n, ?r) ^ Relative(?r) ^ hasRelative(?p, ?r) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ recognizedEvent(?e, ?o)`

        <rdfs:label>PD patient recognized event observation</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 `PDpatientFallingEventObservation(?e) ^ RecognizedEvent(?e) ^ hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ PD_Patient(?p) -> NotifyNurse(?n1) ^ sendNotification(?n1, true) ^ NotifyRelatives(?n2) ^ sendNotification(?n2, true)`

        <rdfs:subClassOf>
            <owl:Restriction>
                <owl:onProperty rdf:resource="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#hasPersonalHealthRecord"/>

            </owl:Restriction>
        </rdfs:subClassOf>
        <rdfs:comment>PD patient</rdfs:comment>
        <rdfs:label>PD Patient</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 `Patient(?p) ^ hasTremor(?p, true) ^ hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> PD_Patient(?p)`

    <!-- http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PersonalHealthRecord -->

    <owl:Class rdf:about="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PersonalHealthRecord">
        <rdfs:comment>Personal Health Record of a patient</rdfs:comment>
        <rdfs:label>Personal Health Record</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^
Monitored(?p) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^
hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^
containsDopamine(?mp, true) ->
ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

<!-- http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PD_Patient -->

    <owl:Class rdf:about="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PD_Patient">
        <rdfs:subClassOf rdf:resource="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#Patient"/>
        <rdfs:subClassOf>
            <owl:Restriction>
                <owl:onProperty rdf:resource="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#hasPersonalHealthRecord"/>
                <owl:someValuesFrom rdf:resource="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PersonalHealthRecord"/>
            </owl:Restriction>
        </rdfs:subClassOf>
        <rdfs:comment>PD patient</rdfs:comment>
        <rdfs:label>PD Patient</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^
schema:Person(?p) ^
hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^
DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt) ^
swrlb:equal(?dt, "4 hours") ^
hasDosagePlan(?p, ?dp) ^
RecognizedEvent(?re) ^
hasFrequency(?re, ?freq) ^
swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinesiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
swrlb:withinTimeInterval(?re, ?dp) ->
sendNotification(?p, ?doctor) ^
hasNotificationRecipient(?notification, ?doctor)

<!-- http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#RecognizedEvent -->

    <owl:Class rdf:about="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#RecognizedEvent">
        <rdfs:subClassOf rdf:resource="http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/Observation"/>
        <rdfs:comment>An observation of a recognized event for a PD Patient</rdfs:comment>
        <rdfs:label>PD patient recognized event observation</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 `PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ hasPatient(?e, ?p) ^ PatientWithParkinsonDisease(?p) -> NotifyDoctor(?n) ^ hasNotificationFor(?n, ?p)`

<!-- http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#RecognizedEvent -->

    <owl:Class rdf:about="http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#RecognizedEvent">
        <rdfs:subClassOf rdf:resource="http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/Observation"/>
        <rdfs:comment>An observation of a recognized event for a PD Patient</rdfs:comment>
        <rdfs:label>PD patient recognized event observation</rdfs:label>
    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^
Monitored(?p) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^
hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?mp) ^
containsDopamine(?mp, true) ->
ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

    </owl:Class>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Person(?p) ^
    hasTremor(?p, true) ^
    hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
    WalkingActivity(?w) ^
    isMeasuredByDevice(?w, ?d) ^
    SmartWatch(?d) ^
    PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?f) ^
    hasObservationFor(?f, ?p) ->
    sendNotification(?p, ?doctor, "Urgent: PD patient shows tremor, bradykinesia, and falling risk")

    </owl:DatatypeProperty>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 Person(?p) ^
Monitored(?p) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
MedicalPrescription(?prescription) ^
hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?prescription) ^
containsDopamine(?prescription, true) ->
ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

        <rdfs:range rdf:resource="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#boolean"/>
        <rdfs:label xml:lang="en">send notification</rdfs:label>
    </owl:DatatypeProperty>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 ```
Patient(?p) ^
schema:Person(?p) ^
hasParkinsonDisease(?p, true) ^
DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt) ^
swrlb:equal(?dt, "4 hours") ^
hasDosagePlan(?p, ?dp) ^
RecognizedEvent(?re) ^
hasFrequency(?re, ?freq) ^
swrlb:equal(?freq, 4) ^
hasTremor(?p, true) ^
hasBradykinesiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
swrlb:withinTimeInterval(?re, ?dp) ->
sendNotification(?p, ?doctor) ^
hasNotificationRecipient(?notification, ?doctor)
